NAMES  OF  SOLDIERS 


WHO  DIED  IN 


DEFENCE  OF  THE  AMERICAN  UNION, 


INTERRED  IN 


NEW  YORK,  ILLINOIS,  YIRGIXIA,  WEST  VIRGINIA,  MISSOURI, 
AND  THE  TERRITORIES  OF  COLORADO  AND  UTAH. 


Four  hundred  thousand  men, 

The  brave,  the  good,  the  true, 
In  tangled  wood,  in  mountain  glen, 
On  battle  iilain,  in  prison  jicn. 

Lie  dead  fur  nie  and  you  I 
Four  hundred  thousand  of  the  bravo 
Have  made  our  ransomed  soil  their  grave 

For  me  and  you  I 

Good  friend,  for  me  and  you. 


WASHINGTON: 

GOVERNMENT    PRINTING    OFFICE 
1867. 


ROLL  OF  HOXOR. 


GENERAL  ORDERS,  )  Quartermaster  General's  Office, 

No.  52.  )  WasJdngton,  D.  C,  August  27,  1867. 

The  following  report  of  May  27,  18G7,  of  Bvt.'  Lieut.  Col.  James 
M.  Moore,  Assistant  Quartermaster,  U.  S.  Army,  of  the  names  of 
soldiers  interred  in  New  York,  Illinois,  Virginia,  West  Virginia, 
Missouri,  and  the  Territories  of  Colorado  and  Utah,  is  published, 
by  authority  of  the  Secretary  of  War,  for  the  information  of  surviving 
comrades  and  friends. 

D.  H.  EUCKER, 
Acting  Quartermaster  General, 

Bvt.  jUaJ.  Gen' I,  U.  S.  A. 


PEEFACE. 


Assistant  Quartermaster's  Office, 
Depot  of  Washington,  No.  151  North  G  Street, 

Washington,  D.  C,  31ay  27,  1867. 

General:  Herewith  I  have  the  honor  to  transmit,  for  publication, 
records  of  interments  of  United  States  soldiers  in  New  York,  Illinois, 
Virginia,  West  Virginia,  Missouri,  and  the  Territories  of  Colorado 
and  Utah.  They  are  alphabetically  arranged  with  three-letter 
index. 

I  am,  General,  very  respectfully. 

Your  obedient  servant, 

JAS.   M.  MOORE, 
Bvt.  Lt.  Col  &  A,  Q.  iV.,   U.  S.  Army. 

Bvt.  Maj.  Gen'l  D.  H.  Rucker, 

Acting  Quartermaster  General, 

Washington,  D,  C. 


United  States  Soldiers  interred  at  Cypress  Hill  Cemetery^  Long  Island, 
.    New  YorTi,  during  the  Rehellion. 

Those  maxted  thus  (*)  have  been  disinterred  and  removed  by  friends.
